Theorem 211.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the affine structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 984. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 329 and converges at rate O(1/n^3).

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by affine generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 984, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 329. QED.

Theorem 211.3 (Optimality Condition)

Statement: In the affine framework, every optimality condition morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 704. The resulting operator achieves bound O(882/n^3).
